I don't think this would be now *blocked* by spoon, but rather that our choice might a/effect this behaviour.\n\nI am also not sure how well your suggested patch works with explicit constructors, as [@SirYwell](https://github.com/SirYwell) mentioned. To properly handle that the method would need to inspect the type and figure out if it should synthesize one or skip doing that. That feels like something that's easier at the end, when the model is already built, rather than reaching deep into the JDT type in the generic factory method. Maybe that's better in an `endVisit` for TypeDeclaration or something similar?\n\nI am also not a *huge* fan of stuffing a special-purpose helper in that factory method (it does quite a lot already), but we might need the field declarations during model build, so I guess that's hard to avoid? Starting with 3.42.0 this is no longer the case. We have to rely on the way Spoon is handing this: addRecordComponent generate implicit fields. So the patch turns implicit fields into explicit ones before asserting.\n\n```\ndiff --git a/src/test/java/spoon/test/record/CtRecordTest.java b/src/test/java/spoon/test/record/CtRecordTest.java\n--- a/src/test/java/spoon/test/record/CtRecordTest.java\t(revision d0f529172b1782e5ae87fea28c853c3341ced27c)\n+++ b/src/test/java/spoon/test/record/CtRecordTest.java\t(date 1767030168284)\n@@ -88,6 +88,16 @@\n \t\tassertEquals(1, records.size());\n \t\tassertEquals(\"public record MultiParameter(int first, float second) {}\", head(records).toString());\n \n+\t\t// Make them explicit so we can print them (but assert they were implicit initially)\n+\t\tassertThat(head(records)).getFields().allSatisfy(CtElement::isImplicit);\n+\t\thead(records).getFields().forEach(it -> it.accept(new CtScanner() {\n+\t\t\t@Override\n+\t\t\tprotected void enter(CtElement e) {\n+\t\t\t\te.setImplicit(false);\n+\t\t\t}\n+\t\t}));\n+\t\thead(records).getFields().forEach(f -> f.getExtendedModifiers().forEach(em -> em.setImplicit(false)));\n+\n \t\t// test fields\n \t\tassertEquals(\n \t\t\t\tArrays.asList(\n\n```\n\n  \n\nAll reactions\n\nSorry, something went wrong.\n\n### Uh oh!\n\nThere was an error while loading.
